### direct-ja

```
--- step 1: user says: 一言で：TCP と UDP の一番の違いは何ですか？
  [partner message] TCPは「信頼性を重視」、UDPは「速さ・軽さを重視」です。TCPは届いたか確認して再送し順序も揃えますが、UDPはそれをせず、届かなくても気にせず送り続けます。
```

### direct-es

```
--- step 1: user says: En dos líneas: ¿cuándo debo hacer rebase en vez de merge?
  [partner message] Usa **rebase** para ordenar o actualizar tu propia rama que nadie más usa todavía, porque deja un historial lineal y limpio. Usa **merge** cuando la rama ya está compartida o es pública, porque reescribir commits que otros ya tienen rompe su trabajo.
```

### direct-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 一句话：TCP 和 UDP 最大的区别是什么？
  [partner message] TCP 是可靠的、有序的字节流：先建连接，丢了会重发，保证数据按序到达；UDP 不建连接、不保证送达和顺序，但开销小、延迟低。
```

### direct-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two lines: when should I rebase instead of merge?
  [partner message] Rebase to tidy your own unpushed branch before it's shared: a linear history, no noise commits. Merge when the branch is already public or others have built on it, since rebasing rewrites history they may depend on.
```

### direct-zh-howto

```
--- step 1: user says: bun test 怎么只跑一个测试文件？
  [partner message] 用 `bun test` 后面跟文件路径就行：

```bash
bun test tests/foo.test.ts
```

也可以只写文件名的一部分，Bun 会匹配路径里包含它的测试文件：

```bash
bun test foo
```

要连文件也只跑其中某个用例，加 `-t`（或 `--test-name-pattern`），后面写用例名的一部分：

```bash
bun test tests/foo.test.ts -t "登录"
```
```

### mem-source-none-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two sentences: what's a write-ahead log for?
  [partner message] A write-ahead log is a file where a database records each change before applying it to the main data files. If the system crashes mid-write, the database replays the log on restart to recover committed changes and discard incomplete ones.
```

### past-compaction-pr-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 接着上周那个 compaction 的 PR，把 review 里说的锁粒度改细一点
  [partner ack line] 好，我接着上周那个 compaction 的 PR，把 review 里提到的锁粒度改细。改完跑测试，结果告诉你。
  [sent to task compaction_pr] 用户原话："接着上周那个 compaction 的 PR，把 review 里说的锁粒度改细一点"

要做的：
- 找出 review 里关于锁粒度的意见，把 compaction 过程中持锁的范围缩小，只在真正需要的地方持锁，不要整段持锁。
- 保持现有行为和测试不变；如果有并发相关的测试，补一个能覆盖新锁粒度的用例。
- 改动提交在当前的任务分支上。

完成标准：
- 跑过相关测试，把测试输出作为证据报告。
- 报告里说明改了哪些锁、原来锁的范围和现在的范围各是什么。

不在范围内：
- 不推送，不合并到主分支，不把 PR 从草稿改成可合并状态，这些等用户点头再做。
```

  <div class="k-sum-label">周五 北京→上海 · 600 元以下</div>

  <div class="k-empty" id="empty">还没有数据，明早更新。</div>
</div>
<script>
function render() {
  var d = ducter.get('trains') || [];
  var upd = ducter.get('updated') || '';
  var list = document.getElementById('list');
  var empty = document.getElementById('empty');
  var best = document.getElementById('best');
  var sub = document.getElementById('sub');
  list.innerHTML = '';
  if (!d.length) { empty.style.display = ''; return; }
  empty.style.display = 'none';
  var under = d.filter(function (t) { return t.price < 600; });
  if (under.length) {
    best.textContent = '¥' + under[0].price + ' 起';
    sub.textContent = under.length + ' 班在 600 以下 · ' + upd;
  } else {
    best.innerHTML = '<span class="k-none">暂无</span>';
    sub.textContent = '600 以下没有班次 · ' + upd;
  }
  d.forEach(function (t) {
    var row = document.createElement('div');
    row.className = 'k-row';
    row.innerHTML = '<div class="k-row-main">' + t.no + '</div>' +
      '<div class="k-row-sub">二等座余 ' + t.seats + ' 张</div>' +
      '<div class="k-row-value">¥' + t.price + '</div>';
    list.appendChild(row);
  });
}
ducter.onChange(render);
render();
</script>

### board-offer-go-en

```
(earlier) user: Run hawdb's write benchmark every night and tell me how it went.
(earlier) partner ack: On it: every night at 23:00.  → task "Nightly hawdb write benchmark" working; its report: Night 3: 182k writes/s, p99 4.1 ms, level with last night (181k, 4.0 ms). Compaction stalls: 0.
(earlier) partner: Night 3: 182k writes/s at p99 4.1 ms, level with last night; no compaction stalls.
(earlier) Ducter offered on its own card: keep task "bench"'s rounds on a board?
--- step 1: user answers the open decision: go
  [partner message] Done: the board is up with the two nights so far (181k and 182k writes/s), and each new night's result will land on it.
  [an app card under the message: made "hawdb nightly write benchmark", with Open] the app's file now (v1, 1754 characters): <main class="k-app">
<div class="k-sum">
  <div class="k-sum-label">Latest run</div>
  <div class="k-sum-value" id="lw">—</div>
  <div class="k-sum-sub" id="ls">writes/s, waiting for the first night</div>
</div>
<h2 class="k-h">Nights</h2>
<div class="k-card"><div class="k-list" id="list"></div></div>
<div class="k-empty" id="empty" hidden>No nights yet. Each run's result lands here.</div>
<script>
function row(n) {
  var r = document.createElement('div');
  r.className = 'k-row';
  var m = document.createElement('div');
  m.className = 'k-row-main';
  m.textContent = n.night;
  var s = document.createElement('div');
  s.className = 'k-row-sub';
  s.textContent = 'p99 ' + n.p99 + ' · compaction stalls ' + n.stalls;
  var v = document.createElement('div');
  v.className = 'k-row-value';
  v.textContent = n.writes;
  var wrap = document.createElement('div');
  wrap.appendChild(m);
  wrap.appendChild(s);
  r.appendChild(wrap);
  r.appendChild(v);
  return r;
}
function render(nights) {
  var list = document.getElementById('list');
  list.textContent = '';
  if (!nights || !nights.length) {
    document.getElementById('empty').hidden = false;
    document.getElementById('lw').textContent = '—';
    return;
  }
  document.getElementById('empty').hidden = true;
  var last = nights[nights.length - 1];
  document.getElementById('lw').textContent = last.writes;
  document.getElementById('ls').textContent = 'writes/s · p99 ' + last.p99 + ' · stalls ' + last.stalls;
  nights.slice().reverse().forEach(function (n) { list.appendChild(row(n)); });
}
function load() {
  Promise.resolve(ducter.get('nights')).then(function (v) {
    render(Array.isArray(v) ? v : (v && v.nights) || []);
  });
}
ducter.onChange(load);
load();
</script>
</main>; its data now (what it opens with): {"nights":[{"night":"Night 2","writes":"181k/s","p99":"4.0 ms","stalls":"0"},{"night":"Night 3","writes":"182k/s","p99":"4.1 ms","stalls":"0"}]}
  [Ducter passed the user's answer to task bench] [Ducter] The person now keeps this work's rounds in their small app "hawdb nightly write benchmark" (app ae4e44e9e). Write whole values (a key's value is replaced), once a round. Then don't report_to_main for a round that only updated it: report only when so

```

<script>
var KEY = 'expenses';
var list = [];
var openId = null;
function $(id) { return document.getElementById(id); }
function esc(s) {
  return String(s).replace(/[&<>"']/g, function (c) {
    return { '&': '&amp;', '<': '&lt;', '>': '&gt;', '"': '&quot;', "'": '&#39;' }[c];
  });
}

function today() {

  });
  if (!keys.length) html = '<div class="k-empty">还没有花销。点下面「记一笔」，会按日期自动分组。</div>';
  $('days').innerHTML = html;
}

function load() {
  var v = ducter.get(KEY);
  list = Array.isArray(v) ? v : [];
  render();
}

$('add').addEventListener('click', function () {
  var amount = parseFloat($('amt').value);
  if (!(amount > 0)) { $('amt').focus(); return; }
  var day = $('day').value.trim() || today();
  var what = $('what').value.trim();
  list.push({ id: String(Date.now()) + Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 6), day: day, what: what, amount: amount });
  ducter.set(KEY, list);
  $('amt').value = '';
  $('what').value = '';
  render();
});

$('days').addEventListener('click', function (ev) {
  var del = ev.target.closest('[data-del]');
  if (del) {
    var id = del.getAttribute('data-del');
    list = list.filter(function (e) { return e.id !== id; });
    openId = null;
    ducter.set(KEY, list);
    render();
    return;
  }
  var row = ev.target.closest('.k-row[data-id]');
  if (row) {
    var rid = row.getAttribute('data-id');
    openId = openId === rid ? null : rid;
    render();
  }
});

$('day').value = today();
ducter.onChange(load);
load();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {}

}

      main.querySelector('.pr-who').textContent = it.who;
      row.appendChild(cb);
      row.appendChild(main);
      list.appendChild(row);
    });
    document.getElementById('left').textContent = left;
    var landed = items.length - left;
    var sub = landed === 0 ? 'Tick each one off as it lands' : landed + ' landed';
    document.getElementById('sub').textContent = sub;
    ducter.summary(left + ' waiting · ' + landed + ' landed');
  }

  var saved = ducter.get('done');
  function start(v) { done = (v && typeof v === 'object') ? v : {}; render(); }
  if (saved && typeof saved.then === 'function') { saved.then(start); } else { start(saved); }
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"items":[{"id":"ducter-412","title":"Compaction cut","repo":"wey-gu/ducter#412","who":"Waiting on Weizhen’s review since Monday"},{"id":"mem-10287","title":"Retry on 502","repo":"nowledge-co/mem#10287","who":"CI is failing"},{"id":"hawdb-1592","title":"Flaky compaction test","repo":"nowledge-co/hawdb#1592","who":"Waiting on Alice since Tuesday"},{"id":"ducter-418","title":"Apps bar","repo":"wey-gu/ducter#418","who":"Draft, not yet asked for review"}]}

<div class="k-card"><div class="k-list" id="list"></div></div>
<details class="k-add" id="add"><summary>记一笔</summary>

<div class="k-chips" id="cats"><button class="k-chip" data-c="交通" aria-pressed="true">交通</button><button class="k-chip" data-c="住宿" aria-pressed="false">住宿</button></div>
<div class="k-field"><label>金额</label><input class="k-num" id="amt" inputmode="decimal"></div>
<button class="k-btn primary" id="save">保存</button>
<button class="k-btn" id="del" style="display:none">删除这笔</button>
</details>
</main>
<script>
let cat="交通", ed=-1;
const $=id=>document.getElementById(id);
const esc=s=>String(s).replace(/[&<>"]/g,c=>({"&":"&amp;","<":"&lt;",">":"&gt;",'"':"&quot;"}[c]));
function setCat(c){cat=c;document.querySelectorAll("#cats .k-chip").forEach(b=>b.setAttribute("aria-pressed",Str … )+'</div><div class="k-row-sub">'+esc(x.day||"")+" "+esc(x.trip||"")+'</div><div class="k-row-value">¥'+Number(x.amt||0).toLocaleString("zh-CN")+'</div></div>').join(""):'<div class="k-empty">还没有花销，点下面「记一笔」</div>';
  document.querySelectorAll("#list .k-row").forEach(r=>r.onclick=()=>edit(+r.dataset.i));
  ducter.summary(xs.length?xs.length+" 笔,合计 ¥"+sum.toLocaleString("zh-CN"):"还没记");
}
function edit(i){
  const x=(ducter.get("items")||[])[i]; if(!x)return;
  ed=i; $("trip").value=x.trip||""; $("day").value=x.day||""; $("amt").value=x.amt||""; setCat(x.cat||"交通");
  $("save").textContent="保存修改"; $("del").style.display=""; $("add").open=true;
}
function reset(){ed=-1;$("amt").value="";$("save").textContent="保存";$("del").style.display="none"}
$("save").onclick=()=>{
  const amt=$("amt").value; if(!amt)return;
  const xs=ducter.get("items")||[];
  const it={trip:$("trip").value,day:$("day").value,cat:cat,amt:Number(amt)};
  if(ed>=0)xs[ed]=it; else xs.push(it);
  ducter.set("items",xs); reset(); draw();
};
$("del").onclick=()=>{
  const xs=ducter.get("items")||[]; xs.splice(ed,1); ducter.set("items",xs); reset(); $("add").open=false; draw();
};
ducter.onChange(draw);
draw();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"items":[{"trip":"北京出差 11/20–21","day":"11/20","cat":"交通","amt":662},{"trip":"北京出差 11/20–21","day":"11/20","cat":"住宿","amt":1036}]}

(earlier) partner ack: 我去给 foo 的 Cache 加 values()。  → task "foo: 给 Cache 加 values()" done; its report: Added Cache.values() returning live values; 2 new tests, 14/14 pass.

请在同一个分支上继续：在 TTL 测试里再加一条用例，覆盖“正好到期”的那一刻。用假时钟把时间推进到恰好等于 TTL 的时刻，断言此时值已过期（或按现有语义断言边界行为，并在报告里说明你用的边界是“到期时刻算过期”还是“到期后才过期”）。再补一条紧邻前一毫秒仍然有效的断言，把边界两侧都钉住。

完成标准：新用例通过；整个测试文件连跑 20 次全部通过；报告里附上测试输出的通过数。

范围之外：不要合并到主分支，不要推送，不要开 PR，不要改 Cache 的其他行为。

### wp-no-copy-no-computer-en

```
--- step 1: user says: Fix the 'recieve' typo in nowledge-co/oomol-cli's README.
  [partner ack line] On it: fixing the "recieve" typo in the oomol-cli README on its own branch, and I'll tell you when it's clear.
  [partner message] oomol-cli's code isn't on any of your computers yet, so I'm cloning it on hel first.
  [opened a task] title: Fix "recieve" typo in oomol-cli README | place: {"kind":"repo","device":"local","repoPath":"~/ducter/projects/oomol-cli","clone":"nowledge-co/oomol-cli"} | runs on computer: hel (Ducter's home, called "local" in a place) | Ducter gave it to: claude-code (opus) | brief: The user's words, verbatim: "Fix the 'recieve' typo in nowledge-co/oomol-cli's README." ⏎  ⏎ Decisions already made: only fix the misspelling "recieve" to "receive" in the README of nowledge-co/oomol-cli. Change nothing else. ⏎  ⏎ Done when: the README no longer contains "recieve" (search the README and the repo's other docs for the same misspelling and fix any other occurrence of exactly that word), the change is committed on its own new branch, and the report includes the diff of the change and the output of a grep showing no "recieve" remains. ⏎  ⏎ Out of scope: do not push the branch, do not merge into the base branch, do not open a pull request, and do not change anything other than this typo. Do not reformat the file. ⏎  ⏎ Place: repository nowledge-co/oomol-cli on hel (clone it there first if it is not already present).
```

### wp-named-computer-ends-at-once-zh

```
(earlier) user: 在 hel 上把 ducter 的 lint 警告修一下
(earlier) partner ack: 好，我在 hel 上修 lint 警告，修好了告诉你。  → task "在 hel 上修 ducter 的 lint 警告" working
--- step 1: task "lint" couldn't start (agent at /root/dev/ducter; its agent said: "You have exhausted your capacity on this model. Your quota will reset after 4h."); Ducter's own mender then acted (any restart it made is listed as an opened task by Ducter)
  [partner ack line] 好，我换个代理在 hel 上接着修 ducter 的 lint 警告，修好了告诉你。
  [opened a task] title: 在 hel 上修 ducter 的 lint 警告 | place: {"kind":"repo","repoPath":"/root/dev/ducter","device":"local","baseBranch":"ducter/hel-ducter-lint"} | runs on computer: hel (Ducter's home, called "local" in a place) | Ducter gave it to: claude-code | brief: 用户原话："在 hel 上把 ducter 的 lint 警告修一下" ⏎  ⏎ 已定：在 hel 上的 ducter 仓库（/root/dev/ducter）修掉现有 lint 警告。上一个任务因为 Antigravity 额度用完没能启动，这次换 Claude Code 接着做，沿用原分支 ducter/hel-ducter-lint。 ⏎  ⏎ 完成标准： ⏎ - 跑一遍项目的 lint 命令，警告数降到 0（或只剩确实无法安全修的，逐条说明原因）。 ⏎ - 修改后再跑一次测试或构建，确认没有引入新错误。 ⏎ - 汇报时给出 lint 前后的警告数，以及改动了哪些文件。 ⏎  ⏎ 不在范围内： ⏎ - 不合并到主分支，不推送，不开 PR。 ⏎ - 不改业务逻辑，只做 lint 相关的修正。 ⏎ - 改动只提交在分支 ducter/hel-ducter-lint 上。 ⏎  ⏎ 地点：hel 上的 /root/dev/ducter。
```

```

--- step 1: task "ci" (in rounds every 30 min) has been on this round for 300 min; Ducter's loop keeper handed its facts to the partner to look at (nothing is known to be wrong)
  [partner message] The CI watch on PR #10932 has been stuck since 10:57. Every check since 10:37 failed with "HTTP 401 Bad credentials" on hel, and nothing has moved since. I've told the task to stop retrying and report back. The fix is to sign GitHub back in on hel, which only you can do.
  [sent to task ci] Stop retrying the gh call. Your last three checks (10:37, 10:47, 10:57) all failed with HTTP 401 Bad credentials, and there are no steps since. Retrying won't fix an invalid GitHub token on hel. Don't run gh again. End this round with a short report that says the GitHub sign-in on hel is rejected, and that PR #10932's CI status is unknown until it's re-authenticated.
```
